```text
{"success": true, "name": "test-driven-development", "description": "TDD: enforce RED-GREEN-REFACTOR, tests before code.", "tags": ["testing", "tdd", "development", "quality", "red-green-refactor"], "related_skills": ["systematic-debugging", "writing-plans", "subagent-driven-development"], "content": "---\nname: test-driven-development\ndescription: \"TDD: enforce RED-GREEN-REFACTOR, tests before code.\"\nversion: 1.1.0\nauthor: Hermes Agent (adapted from obra/superpowers)\nlicense: MIT\nplatforms: [linux, macos, windows]\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    tags: [testing, tdd, development, quality, red-green-refactor]\n    related_skills: [systematic-debugging, writing-plans, subagent-driven-development]\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development (TDD)\n\n## Overview\n\nWrite the test first. Watch it fail. Write minimal code to pass.\n\n**Core principle:** If you didn't watch the test fail, you don't know if it tests the right thing.\n\n**Violating the letter of the rules is violating the spirit of the rules.**\n\n## When to Use\n\n**Always:**\n- New features\n- Bug fixes\n- Refactoring\n- Behavior changes\n\n**Exceptions (ask the user first):**\n- Throwaway prototypes\n- Generated code\n- Configuration files\n\nThinking \"skip TDD just this once\"? Stop. That's rationalization.\n\n## The Iron Law\n\n``​`\nNO PRODUCTION CODE WITHOUT A FAILING TEST FIRST\n``​`\n\nWrite code before the test? Delete it. Start over.\n\n**No exceptions:**\n- Don't keep it as \"reference\"\n- Don't \"adapt\" it while writing tests\n- Don't look at it\n- Delete means delete\n\nImplement fresh from tests. Period.\n\n## Red-Green-Refactor Cycle\n\n### RED — Write Failing Test\n\nWrite one minimal test showing what should happen.\n\n**Good test:**\n``​`python\ndef test_retries_failed_operations_3_times():\n    attempts = 0\n    def operation():\n        nonlocal attempts\n        attempts += 1\n        if attempts < 3:\n            raise Exception('fail')\n        return 'success'\n\n    result = retry_operation(operation)\n\n    assert result == 'success'\n    assert attempts == 3\n``​`\nClear name, tests real behavior, one thing.\n\n**Bad test:**\n``​`python\ndef test_retry_works():\n    mock = MagicMock()\n    mock.side_effect = [Exception(), Exception(), 'success']\n    result = retry_operation(mock)\n    assert result == 'success'  # What about retry count? Timing?\n``​`\nVague name, tests mock not real code.\n\n**Requirements:**\n- One behavior per test\n- Clear descriptive name (\"and\" in name? Split it)\n- Real code, not mocks (unless truly unavoidable)\n- Name describes behavior, not implementation\n\n### Verify RED — Watch It Fail\n\n**MANDATORY. Never skip.**\n\n``​`bash\n# Use terminal tool to run the specific test\npytest tests/test_feature.py::test_specific_behavior -v\n``​`\n\nConfirm:\n- Test fails (not errors from typos)\n- Failure message is expected\n- Fails because the feature is missing\n\n**Test passes immediately?** You're testing existing behavior. Fix the test.\n\n**Test errors?** Fix the error, re-run until it fails correctly.\n\n### GREEN — Minimal Code\n\nWrite the simplest code to pass the test. Nothing more.\n\n**Good:**\n``​`python\ndef add(a, b):\n    return a + b  # Nothing extra\n``​`\n\n**Bad:**\n``​`python\ndef add(a, b):\n    result = a + b\n    logging.info(f\"Adding {a} + {b} = {result}\")  # Extra!\n    return result\n``​`\n\nDon't add features, refactor other code, or \"improve\" beyond the test.\n\n**Cheating is OK in GREEN:**\n- Hardcode return values\n- Copy-paste\n- Duplicate code\n- Skip edge cases\n\nWe'll fix it in REFACTOR.\n\n### Verify GREEN — Watch It Pass\n\n**MANDATORY.**\n\n``​`bash\n# Run the specific test\npytest tests/test_feature.py::test_specific_behavior -v\n\n# Then run ALL tests to check for regressions\npytest tests/ -q\n``​`\n\nConfirm:\n- Test passes\n- Other tests still pass\n- Output pristine (no errors, warnings)\n\n**Test fails?** Fix the code, not the test.\n\n**Other tests fail?** Fix regressions now.\n\n### REFACTOR — Clean Up\n\nAfter green only:\n- Remove duplication\n- Improve names\n- Extract helpers\n- Simplify expressions\n\nKeep tests green throughout. Don't add behavior.\n\n**If tests fail during refactor:** Undo immediately. Take smaller steps.\n\n### Repeat\n\nNext failing test for next behavior. One cycle at a time.\n\n## Why Order Matters\n\n**\"I'll write tests after to verify it works\"**\n\nTests written after code pass immediately. Passing immediately proves nothing:\n- Might test the wrong thing\n- Might test implementation, not behavior\n- Might miss edge cases you forgot\n- You never saw it catch the bug\n\nTest-first forces you to see the test fail, proving it actually tests something.\n\n**\"I already manually tested all the edge cases\"**\n\nManual testing is ad-hoc. You think you tested everything but:\n- No record of what you tested\n- Can't re-run when code changes\n- Easy to forget cases under pressure\n- \"It worked when I tried it\" ≠ comprehensive\n\nAutomated tests are systematic. They run the same way every time.\n\n**\"Deleting X hours of work is wasteful\"**\n\nSunk cost fallacy. The time is already gone. Your choice now:\n- Delete and rewrite with TDD (high confidence)\n- Keep it and add tests after (low confidence, likely bugs)\n\nThe \"waste\" is keeping code you can't trust.\n\n**\"TDD is dogmatic, being pragmatic means adapting\"**\n\nTDD IS pragmatic:\n- Finds bugs before commit (faster than debugging after)\n- Prevents regressions (tests catch breaks immediately)\n- Documents behavior (tests show how to use code)\n- Enables refactoring (change freely, tests catch breaks)\n\n\"Pragmatic\" shortcuts = debugging in production = slower.\n\n**\"Tests after achieve the same goals — it's spirit not ritual\"**\n\nNo. Tests-after answer \"What does this do?\" Tests-first answer \"What should this do?\"\n\nTests-after are biased by your implementation. You test what you built, not what's required. Tests-first force edge case discovery before
```

```text
{"content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\nimport { getCurrentSession } from '@/lib/auth';\nimport { auditLog } from '@/lib/audit';\nimport { prisma } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { summarizeCompletedWorkout } from '@/lib/workout-session';\nimport { CompleteWorkoutSchema } from '@/lib/validations/workout-session';\n\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ sessionId: string }> }) {\n  const authSession = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!authSession) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const { sessionId } = await params;\n  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);\n  const parsed = CompleteWorkoutSchema.safeParse(body);\n  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid workout completion payload' }, { status: 400 });\n\n  const existing = await prisma.workoutSession.findFirst({\n    where: { id: sessionId, userId: authSession.userId },\n    include: { exercises: { include: { sets: true } } },\n  });\n  if (!existing) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Workout session not found' }, { status: 404 });\n\n  const exerciseIds = new Set(existing.exercises.map((exercise) => exercise.id));\n  const setIds = new Set(existing.exercises.flatMap((exercise) => exercise.sets.map((set) => set.id)));\n\n  for (const exercise of parsed.data.exercises) {\n    if (!exerciseIds.has(exercise.exerciseSessionId)) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Exercise does not belong to this workout session' }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n    for (const set of exercise.sets) {\n      if (!setIds.has(set.setSessionId)) {\n        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Set does not belong to this workout session' }, { status: 400 });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const summary = summarizeCompletedWorkout(parsed.data);\n\n  const updated = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {\n    for (const exercise of parsed.data.exercises) {\n      await tx.exerciseSession.update({\n        where: { id: exercise.exerciseSessionId },\n        data: { actualRpe: exercise.actualRpe, notes: exercise.notes },\n      });\n\n      for (const set of exercise.sets) {\n        await tx.setSession.update({\n          where: { id: set.setSessionId },\n          data: {\n            completed: set.completed,\n            actualWeightLb: set.actualWeightLb,\n            actualReps: set.actualReps,\n          },\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    return tx.workoutSession.update({\n      where: { id: sessionId },\n      data: {\n        status: summary.status,\n        completedAt: summary.completedAt,\n        completedVolume: summary.completedVolume,\n        energyScore: summary.energyScore,\n        performanceScore: summary.performanceScore,\n        sorenessScore: summary.sorenessScore,\n        notes: summary.notes,\n      },\n      include: {\n        exercises: {\n          orderBy: { orderIndex: 'asc' },\n          include: { sets: { orderBy: { setNumber: 'asc' } } },\n        },\n      },\n    });\n  });\n\n  await auditLog({\n    userId: authSession.userId,\n    entityType: 'WorkoutSession',\n    entityId: updated.id,\n    action: 'update',\n    oldValueJson: existing,\n    newValueJson: updated,\n  });\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ workoutSession: updated });\n}\n","path":"/opt/data/projects/healthos/app/api/workouts/sessions/[sessionId]/complete/route.ts"}
```

```text
{"content": "     1|export type DailyCalorieInputs = {\n     2|  caloriesIn: number;\n     3|  activeCalories?: number | null;\n     4|  restingCalories?: number | null;\n     5|  tdeeFallbackCalories?: number | null;\n     6|};\n     7|\n     8|export function caloriesOut(input: Omit<DailyCalorieInputs, 'caloriesIn'>): number {\n     9|  const active = input.activeCalories ?? 0;\n    10|  const resting = input.restingCalories ?? 0;\n    11|  const watchTotal = active + resting;\n    12|  if (watchTotal > 0) return watchTotal;\n    13|  return input.tdeeFallbackCalories ?? 0;\n    14|}\n    15|\n    16|export function dailyNetCalories(input: DailyCalorieInputs): number {\n    17|  return input.caloriesIn - caloriesOut(input);\n    18|}\n    19|\n    20|export function sevenDayBalance(days: DailyCalorieInputs[]): number {\n    21|  return days.reduce((sum, day) => sum + dailyNetCalories(day), 0);\n    22|}\n    23|\n    24|export function estimatedPoundsChange(kcalBalance: number): number {\n    25|  return kcalBalance / 3500;\n    26|}\n    27|\n    28|export function calorieBalanceLabel(kcalBalance: number): string {\n    29|  if (kcalBalance < 0) return `${Math.abs(kcalBalance).toLocaleString()} kcal deficit`;\n    30|  if (kcalBalance > 0) return `${kcalBalance.toLocaleString()} kcal surplus`;\n    31|  return '0 kcal balance';\n    32|}\n    33|", "total_lines": 32, "file_size": 1095, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
```

### assistant

```text
Kept going. Two major slices are now done and committed.

## What I just built

### 1. Persisted workout sessions

Before: live workout screen was client-only checkboxes.

Now:

- starting a workout creates a real `WorkoutSession`
- creates nested `ExerciseSession` records
- creates nested `SetSession` records
- live screen logs:
  - set completed / not completed
  - actual weight
  - actual reps
  - exercise RPE
  - workout quality scores
  - notes
- finishing workout persists the session
- partial workouts are allowed and saved accurately
- completed volume is calculated
- audit log entry is written

Commit:

``​`text
977db97 feat: persist workout sessions
``​`

### 2. Real dashboard metrics

Before: Today dashboard cards were placeholders.

Now dashboard queries DB and shows:

- workouts completed in rolling 7 days
- workout target
- 7-day calorie balance
- estimated weight delta from calorie balance
- 7-day average weight
- 7-day average sleep
- today’s macros

Also fixed an important behavior: don’t invent a calorie deficit for a day with no calorie/food data.

Commit:

``​`text
6c5a95a feat: show real dashboard metrics
``​`

## Verification

Passed:

``​`bash
npm run test
npm run typecheck
npm run build
``​`

Current tests:

``​`text
17 passing
``​`

Latest commits:

``​`text
6c5a95a feat: show real dashboard metrics
977db97 feat: persist workout sessions
189fa4f docs: add project status
8a0a5e1 feat: add seed workout preview and live flow
1da4355 feat: add initial dashboard logging UI
``​`

## What’s next

Next highest-leverage slice:

1. **Meal edit / undo / delete**
   - quick meal log already saves
   - next it needs correction flow:
     - edit calories/protein/carbs/fat
     - soft-delete meal
     - undo toast behavior
     - audit log every edit/delete

2. **Goals/settings UI**
   - editable targets
   - date-effective goal history
   - current target cards use real values once configured

3. **Google Sheets backup**
   - export all tables to one Sheet with tabs
   - include `AuditLog`
   - manual export button first
   - nightly cron after deploy

4. **Remote deployment**
   - still blocked until `gh` and Railway auth are available in this runtime. Local repo is clean and buildable.
```

```text
{"content": "import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\nimport { getCurrentSession } from '@/lib/auth';\nimport { prisma } from '@/lib/db';\n\n// GET /api/meals?date=YYYY-MM-DD  (defaults to today UTC)\nexport async function GET(request: NextRequest) {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const dateParam = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('date');\n  const date = dateParam ? new Date(`${dateParam}T00:00:00.000Z`) : startOfUtcDay(new Date());\n\n  const meals = await prisma.meal.findMany({\n    where: { userId: session.userId, deletedAt: null, date },\n    orderBy: { loggedAt: 'asc' },\n  });\n\n  const totals = meals.reduce(\n    (sum, meal) => ({\n      calories: sum.calories + meal.calories,\n      proteinG: sum.proteinG + meal.proteinG,\n      carbsG: sum.carbsG + meal.carbsG,\n      fatG: sum.fatG + meal.fatG,\n    }),\n    { calories: 0, proteinG: 0, carbsG: 0, fatG: 0 },\n  );\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ meals, totals });\n}\n\nfunction startOfUtcDay(date: Date): Date {\n  return new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth(), date.getUTCDate()));\n}\n", "path": "/opt/data/projects/healthos/app/api/meals/route.ts"}
```

NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ mealId: string }> }) {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const { mealId } = await params;\n  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);\n  const parsed = EditMealSchema.safeParse(body);\n  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid meal edit payload', details: parsed.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });\n\n  const existing = await prisma.meal.findFirst({ where: { id: mealId, userId: session.userId, deletedAt: null } });\n  if (!existing) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Meal not found' }, { status: 404 });\n\n  const updated = await prisma.meal.update({\n    where: { id: mealId },\n    data: {\n      ...parsed.data,\n      estimationSource: 'manual',\n    },\n  });\n\n  await auditLog({\n    userId: session.userId,\n    entityType: 'Meal',\n    entityId: mealId,\n    action: 'update',\n    oldValueJson: existing,\n    newValueJson: updated,\n  });\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ meal: updated });\n}\n\n// DELETE /api/meals/[mealId]  \u2014 soft-delete (sets deletedAt)\nexport async function DELETE(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ mealId: string }> }) {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const { mealId } = await params;\n  const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));\n  const parsed = DeleteMealSchema.safeParse(body);\n  // reason is {"content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState, useTransition } from 'react';\n\ntype MealCategory = 'breakfast' | 'lunch' | 'dinner' | 'snack';\n\ntype Meal = {\n  id: string;\n  description: string;\n  mealCategory: MealCategory;\n  loggedAt: string;\n  calories: number;\n  proteinG: number;\n  carbsG: number;\n  fatG: number;\n  estimationSource: string;\n};\n\ntype Totals = { calories: number; proteinG: number; carbsG: number; fatG: number };\n\nfunction defaultCategory(): MealCategory {\n  const hour = new Date().getHours();\n  if (hour < 11) return 'breakfast';\n  if (hour < 15) return 'lunch';\n  if (hour < 21) return 'dinner';\n  return 'snack';\n}\n\nexport default function MealsPage() {\n  const [meals, setMeals] = useState<Meal[]>([]);\n  const [totals, setTotals] = useState<Totals>({ calories: 0, proteinG: 0, carbsG: 0, fatG: 0 });\n  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [toast, setToast] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [undoPending, setUndoPending] = useState<{ mealId: string; meal: Meal } | null>(null);\n\n  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n\n  const fetchMeals = useCallback(async () => {\n    const response = await fetch(`/api/meals?date=${today}`);\n    if (!response.ok) { setLoadError('Could not load meals.'); return; }\n    const data = await response.json();\n    setMeals(data.meals);\n    setTotals(data.totals);\n  }, [today]);\n\n  useEffect(() => { fetchMeals(); }, [fetchMeals]);\n\n  function showToast(msg: string) {\n    setToast(msg);\n    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 3500);\n  }\n\n  async function deleteMeal(meal: Meal) {\n    const response = await fetch(`/api/meals/${meal.id}`, { method: 'DELETE', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({}) });\n    if (!response.ok) { showToast('Could not delete meal.'); return; }\n    setMeals((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== meal.id));\n    setUndoPending({ mealId: meal.id, meal });\n    showToast('Meal deleted. Tap Undo within 30 s to restore.');\n    setTimeout(() => setUndoPending(null), 30000);\n    await fetchMeals();\n  }\n\n  async function undoDelete() {\n    if (!undoPending) return;\n    // Soft-delete undo = re-create the meal with same data (patch deletedAt back to null isn't exposed, so we re-log it)\n    const response = await fetch('/api/meals/estimate-and-log', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        description: undoPending.meal.description,\n        category: undoPending.meal.mealCategory,\n        loggedAt: undoPending.meal.loggedAt,\n        // Pass override macros so it doesn't re-estimate via LLM\n        overrideMacros: {\n          calories: undoPending.meal.calories,\n          proteinG: undoPending.meal.proteinG,\n          carbsG: undoPending.meal.carbsG,\n          fatG: undoPending.meal.fatG,\n        },\n      }),\n    });\n    if (!response.ok) { showToast('Could not restore meal.'); return; }\n    setUndoPending(null);\n    showToast('Meal restored.');\n    await fetchMeals();\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"container\" style={{ padding: '14px 0 40px' }}>\n      <h1 style={{ fontSize: 40, letterSpacing: '-0.05em', marginBottom: 6 }}>Meals</h1>\n\n      <div style={{ background: '#111827', color: '#fff', borderRadius: 22, padding: 18, marginBottom: 18 }}>\n        <p style={{ margin: '0 0 6px', color: '#9ca3af', fontSize:

```text
{"content": "import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\nimport { getCurrentSession } from '@/lib/auth';\nimport { auditLog } from '@/lib/audit';\nimport { prisma } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { GoalPeriodSchema } from '@/lib/validations/goal-period';\n\n// GET /api/goals  \u2014 returns current active goal + history\nexport async function GET() {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const today = startOfUtcDay(new Date());\n\n  const [current, history] = await Promise.all([\n    prisma.goalPeriod.findFirst({\n      where: { userId: session.userId, startsAt: { lte: today }, OR: [{ endsAt: null }, { endsAt: { gte: today } }] },\n      orderBy: { startsAt: 'desc' },\n    }),\n    prisma.goalPeriod.findMany({\n      where: { userId: session.userId },\n      orderBy: { startsAt: 'desc' },\n      take: 20,\n    }),\n  ]);\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ current, history });\n}\n\n// POST /api/goals  \u2014 create a new goal period (closes previous open one)\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);\n  const parsed = GoalPeriodSchema.safeParse(body);\n  if (!parsed.success) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid goal payload', details: parsed.error.flatten() }, { status: 400 });\n\n  const startsAt = new Date(`${parsed.data.startsAt}T00:00:00.000Z`);\n  const endsAt = parsed.data.endsAt ? new Date(`${parsed.data.endsAt}T00:00:00.000Z`) : null;\n\n  const goal = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Close any previously open goal ending the day before new one starts\n    const previousOpen = await tx.goalPeriod.findFirst({\n      where: { userId: session.userId, endsAt: null },\n      orderBy: { startsAt: 'desc' },\n    });\n    if (previousOpen) {\n      const closeAt = new Date(startsAt);\n      closeAt.setUTCDate(closeAt.getUTCDate() - 1);\n      await tx.goalPeriod.update({ where: { id: previousOpen.id }, data: { endsAt: closeAt } });\n    }\n\n    return tx.goalPeriod.create({\n      data: {\n        userId: session.userId,\n        startsAt,\n        endsAt,\n        phaseLabel: parsed.data.phaseLabel ?? 'recomp',\n        caloriesTarget: parsed.data.caloriesTarget,\n        proteinTargetG: parsed.data.proteinTargetG,\n        carbsTargetG: parsed.data.carbsTargetG,\n        fatTargetG: parsed.data.fatTargetG,\n        workoutTargetRolling7D: parsed.data.workoutTargetRolling7D ?? 4,\n        sleepTargetMinutes: parsed.data.sleepTargetMinutes ?? 420,\n        tdeeFallbackCalories: parsed.data.tdeeFallbackCalories,\n        notes: parsed.data.notes,\n      },\n    });\n  });\n\n  await auditLog({ userId: session.userId, entityType: 'GoalPeriod', entityId: goal.id, action: 'create', newValueJson: goal });\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ goal }, { status: 201 });\n}\n\nfunction startOfUtcDay(date: Date): Date {\n  return new Date(Date.UTC(date.getUTCFullYear(), date.getUTCMonth(), date.getUTCDate()));\n}\n", "path": "/opt/data/projects/healthos/app/api/goals/route.ts"}
```

```text
{"content": "import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';\nimport { getCurrentSession } from '@/lib/auth';\nimport { prisma } from '@/lib/db';\nimport { runBackup } from '@/lib/backup';\n\nexport async function POST() {\n  const session = await getCurrentSession();\n  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n\n  // Find the most recent successful backup sheet ID so we re-use it\n  const lastExport = await prisma.backupExportLog.findFirst({\n    where: { userId: session.userId, status: 'success' },\n    orderBy: { startedAt: 'desc' },\n  });\n\n  const log = await prisma.backupExportLog.create({\n    data: { userId: session.userId, exportType: 'manual', status: 'started' },\n  });\n\n  try {\n    const result = await runBackup(session.userId, lastExport?.googleSheetId ?? null);\n    await prisma.backupExportLog.update({\n      where: { id: log.id },\n      data: {\n        status: 'success',\n        googleSheetId: result.spreadsheetId,\n        rowCountsJson: result.rowCounts,\n        completedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    });\n    return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, url: result.url, rowCounts: result.rowCounts, exportedAt: result.exportedAt });\n  } catch (error) {\n    const msg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    await prisma.backupExportLog.update({ where: { id: log.id }, data: { status: 'failed', error: msg, completedAt: new Date() } });\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Backup failed', detail: msg }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n", "path": "/opt/data/projects/healthos/app/api/backup/route.ts"}
```

```text
{"content": "#!/usr/bin/env node\n/**\n * Nightly HealthOS backup script.\n * Called by Railway cron or Hermes cron job.\n * Requires DATABASE_URL and SESSION_SECRET env vars.\n * Uses GOOGLE_SHEETS_TOKEN_PATH and GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET_PATH\n * (defaults to /opt/data/google-accounts/personal/...)\n */\nimport { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';\nimport { runBackup } from '../lib/backup.js';\n\nconst prisma = new PrismaClient();\n\nasync function main() {\n  const users = await prisma.user.findMany({ select: { id: true, username: true } });\n  if (users.length === 0) { console.log('No users found, skipping backup.'); return; }\n\n  for (const user of users) {\n    console.log(`Backing up user: ${user.username}`);\n    const lastExport = await prisma.backupExportLog.findFirst({\n      where: { userId: user.id, status: 'success' },\n      orderBy: { startedAt: 'desc' },\n    });\n\n    const log = await prisma.backupExportLog.create({\n      data: { userId: user.id, exportType: 'nightly', status: 'started' },\n    });\n\n    try {\n      const result = await runBackup(user.id, lastExport?.googleSheetId ?? null);\n      await prisma.backupExportLog.update({\n        where: { id: log.id },\n        data: { status: 'success', googleSheetId: result.spreadsheetId, rowCountsJson: result.rowCounts, completedAt: new Date() },\n      });\n      console.log(`Backup complete for ${user.username}: ${result.url}`);\n      console.log('Row counts:', JSON.stringify(result.rowCounts));\n    } catch (error) {\n      const msg = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n      await prisma.backupExportLog.update({ where: { id: log.id }, data: { status: 'failed', error: msg, completedAt: new Date() } });\n      console.error(`Backup FAILED for ${user.username}:`, msg);\n      process.exitCode = 1;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nmain()\n  .catch((error) => { console.error('Fatal backup error:', error); process.exit(1); })\n  .finally(() => prisma.$disconnect());\n", "path": "/opt/data/projects/healthos/scripts/nightly-backup.ts"}
```

If `gh` is not installed → use \"git-only\" method below (no sudo needed)\n\n---\n\n## Method 1: Git-Only Authentication (No gh, No sudo)\n\nThis works on any machine with `git` installed. No root access needed.\n\n### Option A: HTTPS with Personal Access Token (Recommended)\n\nThis is the most portable method — works everywhere, no SSH config needed.\n\n**Step 1: Create a personal access token**\n\nTell the user to go to: **https://github.com/settings/tokens**\n\n- Click \"Generate new token (classic)\"\n- Give it a name like \"hermes-agent\"\n- Select scopes:\n  - `repo` (full repository access — read, write, push, PRs)\n  - `workflow` (trigger and manage GitHub Actions)\n  - `read:org` (if working with organization repos)\n- Set expiration (90 days is a good default)\n- Copy the token — it won't be shown again\n\n**Step 2: Configure git to store the token**\n\n``​`bash\n# Set up the credential helper to cache credentials\n# \"store\" saves to ~/.git-credentials in plaintext (simple, persistent)\ngit config --global credential.helper store\n\n# Now do a test operation that triggers auth — git will prompt for credentials\n# Username: <their-github-username>\n# Password: REDACTED the personal access token, NOT their GitHub password>\ngit ls-remote https://github.com/<their-username>/<any-repo>.git\n``​`\n\nAfter entering credentials once, they're saved and reused for all future operations.\n\n**Alternative: cache helper (credentials expire from memory)**\n\n``​`bash\n# Cache in memory for 8 hours (28800 seconds) instead of saving to disk\ngit config --global credential.helper 'cache --timeout=28800'\n``​`\n\n**Alternative: set the token directly in the remote URL (per-repo)**\n\n``​`bash\n# Embed token in the remote URL (avoids credential prompts entirely)\ngit remote set-url origin https://<username>:<token>@github.com/<owner>/<repo>.git\n``​`\n\n**Step 3: Configure git identity**\n\n``​`bash\n# Required for commits — set name and email\ngit config --global user.name \"Their Name\"\ngit config --global user.email \"[REDACTED_EMAIL]\"\n``​`\n\n**Step 4: Verify**\n\n``​`bash\n# Test push access (this should work without any prompts now)\ngit ls-remote https://github.com/<their-username>/<any-repo>.git\n\n# Verify identity\ngit config --global user.name\ngit config --global user.email\n``​`\n\n### Option B: SSH Key Authentication\n\nGood for users who prefer SSH or already have keys set up.\n\n**Step 1: Check for existing SSH keys**\n\n``​`bash\nls -la ~/.ssh/id_*.pub 2>/dev/null || echo \"No SSH keys found\"\n``​`\n\n**Step 2: Generate a key if needed**\n\n``​`bash\n# Generate an ed25519 key (modern, secure, fast)\nssh-keygen -t ed25519 -C \"[REDACTED_EMAIL]\" -f ~/.ssh/id_ed25519 -N \"\"\n\n# Display the public key for them to add to GitHub\ncat ~/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub\n``​`\n\nTell the user to add the public key at: **https://github.com/settings/keys**\n- Click \"New SSH key\"\n- Paste the public key content\n- Give it a title like \"hermes-agent-<machine-name>\"\n\n**Step 3: Test the connection**\n\n``​`bash\nssh -T [REDACTED_EMAIL]\n# Expected: \"Hi <username>! You've successfully authenticated...\"\n``​`\n\n**Step 4: Configure git to use SSH for GitHub**\n\n``​`bash\n# Rewrite HTTPS GitHub URLs to SSH automatically\ngit config --global url.\"[REDACTED_EMAIL]:\".insteadOf \"https://github.com/\"\n``​`\n\n**Step 5: Configure git identity**\n\n``​`bash\ngit config --global user.name \"Their Name\"\ngit config --global user.email \"[REDACTED_EMAIL]\"\n``​`\n\n---\n\n## Method 2: gh CLI Authentication\n\nIf `gh` is installed, it handles both API access and git credentials in one step.\n\n### Interactive Browser Login (Desktop)\n\n``​`bash\ngh auth login\n# Select: GitHub.com\n# Select: HTTPS\n# Authenticate via browser\n``​`\n\n### Token-Based Login (Headless / SSH Servers)\n\n``​`bash\necho \"<THEIR_TOKEN>\" | gh auth login --with-token\n\n# Set up git credentials through gh\ngh auth setup-git\n``​`\n\n### Verify\n\n``​`bash\ngh auth status\n``​`\n\n---\n\n## Using the GitHub API Without gh\n\nWhen `gh` is not available, you can still access the full GitHub API using `curl` with a personal access token. This is how the other GitHub skills implement their fallbacks.\n\n### Setting the Token for API Calls\n\n``​`bash\n# Option 1: Export as env var (preferred — keeps it out of commands)\nexport GITHUB_TOKEN=REDACTED Then use in curl calls:\ncurl -s -H \"Authorization: tok

```text
{"content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport pexpect, sys, re, time\n\ncmd = 'gh auth login --hostname github.com --web --scopes repo,workflow,read:org'\nchild = pexpect.spawn(cmd, encoding='utf-8', timeout=None)\nchild.logfile = sys.stdout\n\nwhile True:\n    idx = child.expect([\n        r'What is your preferred protocol.*',\n        r'Authenticate Git with your GitHub credentials\\? \\(Y/n\\)',\n        r'First copy your one-time code:',\n        r'Open this URL to continue in your web browser:',\n        pexpect.EOF,\n        pexpect.TIMEOUT,\n    ], timeout=30)\n    if idx == 0:\n        child.sendline('HTTPS')\n    elif idx == 1:\n        child.sendline('n')\n    elif idx in (2,3):\n        # Let gh print the code/url; then wait until completion.\n        continue\n    elif idx == 4:\n        sys.exit(child.exitstatus or 0)\n    elif idx == 5:\n        # Keep waiting; user may still be approving device code.\n        continue\n","path":"/opt/data/projects/healthos/scripts/gh-auth-pexpect.py"}
```

Cloning Repositories\n\nCloning is pure `git` — works identically either way:\n\n``​`bash\n# Clone via HTTPS (works with credential helper or token-embedded URL)\ngit clone https://github.com/owner/repo-name.git\n\n# Clone into a specific directory\ngit clone https://github.com/owner/repo-name.git ./my-local-dir\n\n# Shallow clone (faster for large repos)\ngit clone --depth 1 https://github.com/owner/repo-name.git\n\n# Clone a specific branch\ngit clone --branch develop https://github.com/owner/repo-name.git\n\n# Clone via SSH (if SSH is configured)\ngit clone [REDACTED_EMAIL]:owner/repo-name.git\n``​`\n\n**With gh (shorthand):**\n\n``​`bash\ngh repo clone owner/repo-name\ngh repo clone owner/repo-name -- --depth 1\n``​`\n\n## 2. Creating Repositories\n\n**With gh:**\n\n``​`bash\n# Create a public repo and clone it\ngh repo create my-new-project --public --clone\n\n# Private, with description and license\ngh repo create my-new-project --private --description \"A useful tool\" --license MIT --clone\n\n# Under an organization\ngh repo create my-org/my-new-project --public --clone\n\n# From existing local directory\ncd /path/to/existing/project\ngh repo create my-project --source . --public --push\n``​`\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n``​`bash\n# Create the remote repo via API\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/user/repos \\\n  -d '{\n    \"name\": \"my-new-project\",\n    \"description\": \"A useful tool\",\n    \"private\": false,\n    \"auto_init\": true,\n    \"license_template\": \"mit\"\n  }'\n\n# Clone it\ngit clone https://github.com/$GH_USER/my-new-project.git\ncd my-new-project\n\n# -- OR -- push an existing local directory to the new repo\ncd /path/to/existing/project\ngit init\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Initial commit\"\ngit remote add origin https://github.com/$GH_USER/my-new-project.git\ngit push -u origin main\n``​`\n\nTo create under an organization:\n\n``​`bash\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/orgs/my-org/repos \\\n  -d '{\"name\": \"my-new-project\", \"private\": false}'\n``​`\n\n### From a Template\n\n**With gh:**\n\n``​`bash\ngh repo create my-new-app --template owner/template-repo --public --clone\n``​`\n\n**With curl:**\n\n``​`bash\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/owner/template-repo/generate \\\n  -d '{\"owner\": \"'\"$GH_USER\"'\", \"name\": \"my-new-app\", \"private\": false}'\n``​`\n\n## 3. Forking Repositories\n\n**With gh:**\n\n``​`bash\ngh repo fork owner/repo-name --clone\n``​`\n\n**With git + curl:**\n\n``​`bash\n# Create the fork via API\ncurl -s -X POST \\\n  -H \"Authorization: token $GITHUB_TOKEN\" \\\n  https://api.github.com/repos/owner/repo-name/forks\n\n# Wait a moment for GitHub to create it, then clone\nsleep 3\ngit clone https://github.com/$GH_USER/repo-name.git\ncd repo-name\n\n# Add the original repo as \"upstream\" remote\ngit remote add upstream https://github.com/owner/repo-name.git\n``​`\n\n### Keeping a Fork in Sync\n\n``​`bash\n# Pure git — works everywhere\ngit fetch upstream\ngit checkout main\ngit merge upstream/main\ngit push origin main\n``​`\n\n**With gh (shortcut):**\n\n``​`bash\ngh repo sync $GH_USER/repo-name\n``​`\n\n## 4.
